ol qotedy lshedy qokar oshkal al okeedy chedy qokain edy
lchedy qokaiin sain olol ol shedy chedy qokchdy kam
tqos kedy okair qokeedy chpor or qokedy olaly daiin arom
shedy edy daiin qokedy ytal okol chedy chedy qotain ol
lckheedy qokain sainol shedy qotedy cheedy qotchdy ol
shedy chedy kol
pain opor lchol shalor lchedy qotaiiin chor chy olary yd
qokaiin ol aly lckhedy okeedy qokain qokedy shedy oteedy
chedy qokain dain sheedy chedy lcheedy cheedy tal qokar
ykor qotaiin okar daiin
daiiin dar san aiin opor or aiin chy chomor kor dan okam
dy ldar sol dan odar chky daiin air dal oraiin ochy ais
ral kydaiin dol chky ydar daldal shdal qochy sor sol som
dolchky ol dy dolchky dal dal ky aldor sar sol shdol dy
qo sol chtaiin dol chky dol chky daiin dy sal rol sair o
ror y sol dain rar shol o
pchar dan paiin sho dysal amramg daiinal chalshol ain y
rar y o oldy shol oldy aiin olshol ol o qo y ror daiir o
qo dol chky old dalchky dy dair dain dal shol y dan o an
rar olol dy y dy y dain chkaiin qo dain ral oshy o chdan
ys daiin o daiis shol olckhy ol sol o qo
paiin pol alaiiin dain o o lkedyl sholshal qoshy chorchy
qodol dy ol ol okain y dolchky shal qochy otain qo ol to
yal ror or oldy daiin chkaiin daiin olckhy yor y dy dam
ol ol ol ckhy dain ololk sol qosol y qo dain oltchy shom
kchy odain tchy dy oldy daiin chty o olcthy ol sain shy
o qosol olchy dair ain o oroldy dy ol ckhy y o y dain ey
shor or olckhy chtain shar olkshy shar ar daiir ykain ky
y ol cthy ckhockhy dy dol chky y dain olkshy sal al an
pain qochy okchey kaiin chpy saiin cheedy chal chor pom
cheedy otchey chedy chedy daiin shar ar cheedy eedy chey
okchey kcheey daiin okedy cheedy dain cheal okshey tchey
okshey kshey odain okshey cheed
pod dainar ochy chfaiin chor taiin cheol shky chal pshey
lokedy dy otshey ror okeedy daiin cheol qokedy otchey
ykeedy dain lokedy qotedy okeedy lotedy dair air ltedy o
lytedy lokedy qoteedy qoteedy ol lokedy qokedy otedy dan
qoteedy daiin qotchdy tchdy dain teedy qokedy ainar kedy
qokshdy qokedy qotshdy okaiin qokedy okchdy
kchor aiin opedy qofchdy olaiin chyol lchedy tair kaiin
qokedy kchdy chyol qokedy kedy lchedy chyol lchedy chedy
okeedy chyol qotedy okedy ol aiin lchedy chedy lchedy an
ain lchedy chedy okeedy keedy an laiin okeedy ykedy an
ain oteedy chedy chedy aiin ykeedy chedy lckhed lshedy
aiis qokedy lchedy qokedy otedy air lshedy lcthes lshedy
okeedy daiin lshedy okeedy cheedy qoteedy
kaiin lsheedy lshedy aiiinal chpol qokeedy qopedy qofedy
ched shol ol ar daiin aiin qokeedy qokchdy qokedy oteedy
shal lchedy daiin qokedy lckhed kched otedy daiin shor
qokedy qokedy shol dain an lcthes qokchdy tedy
kaiin sholol ol cheedy pain pchedy ar qo ain qopedy chey
cheeol qopedy chedy qokeedy chedy dain ain qokedy chey
okeedy ytedy shol chear chedyl dain ain qokeedy oteedy
qotedy qokeedy sholdain lchey sholshol cheear qokeedy
chey qokeedy shor qokeedy aiin qotedy okey chey lchedy
aiin okedy okey chey qoteedy daiin sholar shy kedy
pshy ledy ain paim chedy tom ltchey shol chy ar cthed ey
dain shor qokedy shar an ainolam chedy ytey qokey aiin
qokeedy tedy qotedy chedy ain okey yedy shol cheed cheom
cheo qokeedy air lchedy chedy dain okedy qokey qokedy an
lshedy shed opchdy aiin ain lckhes kshdy qoteedy okedy o
aiin chol shalshal al ais qokeedy otedy qotchdy
tal ain qofedy chkanor y chdain chpor shol olkar qo o am
lain cheeol chol qokey aiin o qokedy chal qotey sheed an
air chal shedy daiin qokshdy shy cheedy eedy daiin ykeom
